# P02 集中专项证据矩阵（Markdown可读版）

> 本文档由 `P02_集中专项证据矩阵.xlsx` 机械转换生成，保留工作簿证据边界。空缺信息统一显示为“未报告”，不作补猜。

## 1. 使用边界

- 候选规模：95篇。
- 本轮仅完成S1–S6检索、去重和题名/摘要初筛。
- 所有候选均为全文待核验；当前证据类别为unresolved、强度为indirect。
- 不得用本矩阵声称不存在既有工作、机制创新成立或性能优越。
- 跨论文性能比较只有在问题、规模、约束、预算、指标和随机协议六维均兼容时才允许标记为DIRECT。

## 2. 项目范围决策

| ID | 范围元素 | 当前决定 | 依据 | 排除项 | 状态 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| D001 | Review scope | Static machine–AGV innovation review; step 4 search and screening only | Frozen protocol P02-SEARCH-v1.0 | No novelty conclusion; no performance ranking | Search complete |
| D002 | Search boundary | S1–S6; 2000-01-01 to 2026-09-02 | OpenAlex, Crossref, Semantic Scholar, arXiv and citation tracing | Chinese databases unavailable in current environment | Logged |
| D003 | Evidence boundary | Metadata and abstract evidence is provisional | Step 4 does not substitute for original full-text extraction | No DIRECT comparison unless six comparability dimensions pass | Applied |

#### 证据边界

- 作者结论：Adaptive Large Neighbourhood Search (ALNS) is a popular metaheuristic with renowned efficiency in solving combinatorial optimisation problems. However, despite 18 years of intensive research into ALNS, the design of an effective adaptive layer for selecting operators to improve the solution remains an open question. In this work, we isolate this problem by formulating it as a Markov Decision Process, in which an agent is rewarded proportionally to the improvement of the incumbent. We propose Graph Reinforcement Learning for Operator Selection (GRLOS), a method based on Deep Reinforcement Learn

Improving the upper bounds of some famous benchmarks is inseparable from the role of these neighbourhood structures. #### 证据边界

- 作者结论：A fast and easily implementable approximation algorithm for the problem of finding a minimum makespan in a job shop is presented. The algorithm is based on a taboo search technique with a specific neighborhood definition which employs a critical path and blocks of operations notions. Computational experiments (up to 2,000 operations) show that the algorithm not only finds shorter makespans than the best approximation approaches but also runs in shorter time. It solves the well-known 10 × 10 hard benchmark problem within 30 seconds on a personal computer.
- 直接证据：No full-text direct evidence extracted in step 4.

#### 证据边界

- 作者结论：The flexible job shop scheduling problem (FJSP) is an NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem, which has wide applications in the real world. The complexity and relevance of the FJSP have led to numerous research works on its modeling and resolution. This paper reviews some of the research of the past 30 years on the problem, by presenting and classifying the different criteria, constraints, configurations and solution approaches that have been considered. Recent emerging topics on complex shop scheduling, multi-criteria optimization and uncertain and dynamic environments are discussed. Fin
